    let MASTER_UNIT={...BASE_MASTER_UNIT};
    
    async function loadUnitMasterOnline(){
      try{
        const {data,error}=await supabaseClient
          .from("unit_master")
          .select("code_unit,egi,code_ellips,active");
    
        if(error) throw error;
    
        (data||[]).forEach(row=>{
          const code=String(row.code_unit||"").trim().toUpperCase();
          if(!code)return;
    
          const old=MASTER_UNIT[code]||{};
          MASTER_UNIT[code]={
            egi:row.egi||old.egi||"",
            ellipse:row.code_ellips||old.ellipse||code,
            active:row.active!==false
          };
        });
      }catch(error){
        console.warn("Unit Master online belum terbaca, memakai master bawaan:",error.message);
      }
    }
    
    let ALL_DATA=[];
    const LOGSHEET_PAGE_SIZE=300;
    let LOGSHEET_VISIBLE_LIMIT=LOGSHEET_PAGE_SIZE;
    let STATUS_MAP=new Map();
    let AUDIT_MAP=new Map();
    let HM_ANOMALY_MAP=new Map();
    let HM_CLEANUP_ROW=null;
    let LOG_STORAGE_ROWS=[];
    let LOG_FT_MAP={};
    let VALID_WHS=new Set();
    let WH_TO_FTS=new Map();
    
    async function loadStorageMaster(){
      if(!window.KPPStorage)throw new Error("Master MT/FT belum termuat.");
      LOG_STORAGE_ROWS=await window.KPPStorage.load(supabaseClient,{activeOnly:false});
      LOG_FT_MAP=window.KPPStorage.ftMap(LOG_STORAGE_ROWS);
      VALID_WHS=new Set(Object.values(LOG_FT_MAP).filter(Boolean));
      WH_TO_FTS=new Map();
      Object.entries(LOG_FT_MAP).forEach(([ft,wh])=>{
        if(!wh)return;
        if(!WH_TO_FTS.has(wh))WH_TO_FTS.set(wh,[]);
        WH_TO_FTS.get(wh).push(ft);
      });
      WH_TO_FTS.forEach(list=>list.sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b)));
    }
    
    function formatAngka(v){ return Number(v||0).toLocaleString("id-ID"); }
    function formatTanggal(v){
      if(!v) return "-";
      const p=String(v).split("-");
      return p.length===3 ? `${p[2]}/${p[1]}/${p[0]}` : v;
    }
    function getWH(item){
      if(item.wh){
        const direct=window.KPPStorage?.normalizeWarehouse(item.wh)||String(item.wh).trim().toUpperCase();
        if(direct)return direct;
      }
      const ft=String(item.fuel_truck||"").trim().toUpperCase();
      return LOG_FT_MAP[ft]||"-";
    }
    
    function getFuelTruck(item){
      const ft=String(item.fuel_truck||"").trim().toUpperCase();
      if(ft)return ft;
      const wh=getWH(item);
      const candidates=WH_TO_FTS.get(wh)||[];
      return candidates.length===1?candidates[0]:"-";
    }
    
    function getFuelman(item){
      return String(
        item.fuelman_name ||
        item.fuelman ||
        ""
      ).trim() || "-";
    }
    
    function duplicateSignature(item){
      const tanggal=String(item.tanggal||"").trim();
      const shift=String(item.shift||"").trim();
      const wh=getWH(item);
      const unit=String(item.unit||"").trim().toUpperCase();
      const fuel=Number(item.fuel);
      const hmRaw=item.hm_akhir;
      const jam=String(item.jam||"").trim();
    
      if(!tanggal || !shift || !unit || !Number.isFinite(fuel) || fuel<=0) return "";
    
      // Paling aman: transaksi dianggap dobel bila HM-nya sama persis.
      // Untuk transaksi web yang punya jam tetapi tidak punya HM, jam dipakai sebagai pengaman.
      if(hmRaw!==null && hmRaw!==undefined && hmRaw!==""){
        const hm=Number(hmRaw);
        if(Number.isFinite(hm)){
          return `HM|${tanggal}|${shift}|${wh}|${unit}|${hm}|${fuel}`;
        }
      }
    
      if(jam){
        return `JAM|${tanggal}|${shift}|${wh}|${unit}|${jam}|${fuel}`;
      }
    
      return "";
    }
    
    function buildStatusMap(data){
      const statusMap=new Map();
      const seen=new Set();
    
      data.forEach((item,index)=>{
        const key=duplicateSignature(item);
        const rowKey=String(item.id ?? `idx-${index}`);
    
        if(key){
          if(seen.has(key)){
            statusMap.set(rowKey,{
              text:"DOUBLE POSTING",
              cls:"status-double",
              reason:"Transaksi sama sudah ada sebelumnya (tanggal, shift, WH, unit, HM/jam, dan QTY sama).",
              kind:"double"
            });
          }else{
            seen.add(key);
          }
        }
      });
    
      return statusMap;
    }
    
    function buildHmAnomalyMap(data){
      const map=new Map();
      const lastValid=new Map();
      const jumpLimit=Math.max(1,Number(document.getElementById("hmJumpLimit")?.value||100));
    
      const sorted=(data||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>{
        const ua=String(a.unit||"").toUpperCase(), ub=String(b.unit||"").toUpperCase();
        if(ua!==ub)return ua.localeCompare(ub);
        const ka=hmReportOrderKey(a);
        const kb=hmReportOrderKey(b);
        return ka.localeCompare(kb);
      });
    
      sorted.forEach(item=>{
        const unit=String(item.unit||"").trim().toUpperCase();
        const rowKey=String(item.id??"");
        if(!unit||!rowKey)return;
    
        if(item.hm_ref_excluded===true){
          map.set(rowKey,{text:"HISTORI HM — TIDAK JADI ACUAN",cls:"status-excluded",reason:item.hm_ref_excluded_reason||"Dikecualikan dari HM reference oleh Admin/GL/Atasan.",kind:"excluded"});
          // Exclusion applies to live references, not report anomaly detection.
          // Continue checking the displayed HM; real anomalies override this hidden label.
        }
    
        if(item.hm_akhir===null||item.hm_akhir===undefined||item.hm_akhir===""){
          map.set(rowKey,{
            text:"HM KOSONG — BISA DIRAPIKAN",
            cls:"status-missing",
            reason:"HM histori kosong. Admin/GL/Atasan dapat mengisi HM histori melalui RAPIKAN HM; hasilnya tetap dikecualikan dari HM reference live.",
            kind:"missing",
            previous_hm:lastValid.get(unit)?.hm ?? null
          });
          return;
        }
        const hm=Number(item.hm_akhir);
        if(!Number.isFinite(hm)){
          map.set(rowKey,{
            text:"HM TIDAK VALID — BISA DIRAPIKAN",
            cls:"status-missing",
            reason:"Nilai HM histori tidak valid. Admin/GL/Atasan dapat merapikannya tanpa menjadikannya HM reference live.",
            kind:"missing",
            previous_hm:lastValid.get(unit)?.hm ?? null
          });
          return;
        }
    
        const prev=lastValid.get(unit)||null;
        if(!prev){
          lastValid.set(unit,{hm,id:item.id,tanggal:item.tanggal});
          return;
        }
    
        const delta=hm-prev.hm;
        // Compare consecutive valid report readings, not a permanently stuck baseline.
        // This is a display calculation only; live HM reference rules remain unchanged.
        lastValid.set(unit,{hm,id:item.id,tanggal:item.tanggal});
        if(delta<0){
          map.set(rowKey,{text:`HM TURUN ${formatAngka(prev.hm)} → ${formatAngka(hm)}`,cls:"status-anomaly",reason:`Indikasi anomali: HM turun ${formatAngka(Math.abs(delta))} dari HM valid sebelumnya ${formatAngka(prev.hm)}.`,kind:"down",previous_hm:prev.hm});
          return;
        }
        if(delta>jumpLimit){
          map.set(rowKey,{text:`HM LONCAT +${formatAngka(delta)}`,cls:"status-anomaly",reason:`Indikasi anomali: kenaikan ${formatAngka(delta)} HM melebihi batas cek ${formatAngka(jumpLimit)} HM.`,kind:"jump",previous_hm:prev.hm});
          return;
        }
    
        lastValid.set(unit,{hm,id:item.id,tanggal:item.tanggal});
      });
    
      return map;
    }
    
    function hmReportOrderKey(item){
      const shift=Number(item.shift);
      const time=String(item.jam||"").slice(0,8);
      // Unknown times follow known readings in the same shift, in ID order.
      // On Shift 2, after-midnight readings follow evening readings.
      const period=!time?"2":shift===2 && time<"06:30"?"1":"0";
      return `${item.tanggal||""}|${shift===1?"1":shift===2?"2":"3"}|${period}|${time}|${String(item.id??"").padStart(20,"0")}`;
    }
    
    function prettyRole(role){
      const r=String(role||"").trim().toLowerCase();
      if(r==="gl") return "GL";
      if(r==="admin") return "ADMIN";
      if(r==="atasan") return "ATASAN";
      if(r==="fuelman") return "FUELMAN";
      return r ? r.toUpperCase() : "USER";
    }
    
    function sameAuditValue(a,b){
      const normalize=v=>{
        if(v===undefined || v===null || v==="") return null;
        if(typeof v==="number") return Number(v);
        return String(v).trim();
      };
    
      return JSON.stringify(normalize(a))===JSON.stringify(normalize(b));
    }
    
    function auditChangedFields(audit){
      const oldData=audit?.old_data||{};
      const newData=audit?.new_data||{};
    
      const fields=[
        ["tanggal","TANGGAL"],
        ["jam","JAM"],
        ["unit","UNIT"],
        ["operator","OPERATOR"],
        ["hm_awal","HM AWAL"],
        ["hm_akhir","HM"],
        ["hm_jalan","HM JALAN"],
        ["fuel","QTY"],
        ["shift","SHIFT"],
        ["fuel_truck","FT"],
        ["wh","WH"],
        ["fuelman_name","FUELMAN"]
      ];
    
      return fields
        .filter(([key])=>!sameAuditValue(oldData[key],newData[key]))
        .map(([,label])=>label);
    }
    
    function buildAuditMap(audits){
      const map=new Map();
    
      (audits||[]).forEach(audit=>{
        if(String(audit.action||"").toUpperCase()!=="UPDATE") return;
    
        const key=String(audit.record_id??"");
        if(!key) return;
    
        // Audit diurutkan ASC, jadi map menyimpan edit terakhir.
        map.set(key,audit);
      });
    
      return map;
    }
    
    function getEditStatus(item){
      const audit=AUDIT_MAP.get(String(item.id??""));
      if(!audit) return null;
    
      const role=prettyRole(audit.changed_by_role);
      const name=String(audit.changed_by_name||"").trim() || "-";
      const fields=auditChangedFields(audit);
    
      const fieldText=fields.length
        ? ` (${fields.slice(0,4).join(", ")}${fields.length>4?", ...":""})`
        : "";
    
      const time=audit.created_at
        ? new Date(audit.created_at).toLocaleString("id-ID",{
            timeZone:"Asia/Jakarta",
            day:"2-digit",
            month:"2-digit",
            year:"numeric",
            hour:"2-digit",
            minute:"2-digit"
          })
        : "-";
    
      return {
        text:`EDIT ${role}: ${name}${fieldText}`,
        cls:"status-edit",
        reason:`Terakhir diedit oleh ${role} ${name} pada ${time}. Kolom berubah: ${fields.join(", ")||"tidak terdeteksi"}.`,
        kind:"edit"
      };
    }
    
    function getDataErrorStatus(item){
      const problems=[];
      const unit=String(item.unit||"").trim().toUpperCase();
      const wh=getWH(item);
      const shift=Number(item.shift);
      const fuel=Number(item.fuel);
    
      if(!item.tanggal) problems.push("TANGGAL KOSONG");
      if(!unit) problems.push("CODE UNIT KOSONG");
      else if(!MASTER_UNIT[unit]) problems.push("UNIT TIDAK ADA DI MASTER");
      if(!VALID_WHS.has(wh)) problems.push("WH TIDAK DIKENAL");
      if(shift!==1 && shift!==2) problems.push("SHIFT TIDAK VALID");
      if(!Number.isFinite(fuel) || fuel<=0) problems.push("QTY TIDAK VALID");
    
      // HM kosong / HM lebih kecil bukan error.
      if(problems.length===0) return null;
    
      return {
        text:`ERROR: ${problems.join(" + ")}`,
        cls:"status-error",
        reason:problems.join(", "),
        kind:"error"
      };
    }
    
    function getHmWarning(item){
      const awal=item.hm_awal;
      const akhir=item.hm_akhir;
    
      if(
        awal===null || awal===undefined || awal==="" ||
        akhir===null || akhir===undefined || akhir===""
      ){
        return null;
      }
    
      const hmAwal=Number(awal);
      const hmAkhir=Number(akhir);
    
      if(
        Number.isFinite(hmAwal) &&
        Number.isFinite(hmAkhir) &&
        hmAkhir<hmAwal
      ){
        return {
          text:`HM DI BAWAH ACUAN (${formatAngka(hmAkhir)} < ${formatAngka(hmAwal)})`,
          cls:"status-hm",
          reason:`HM Saat Isi ${formatAngka(hmAkhir)} lebih kecil dari HM acuan ${formatAngka(hmAwal)}. Data tetap sah; HM ini tidak menurunkan acuan HM berikutnya.`,
          kind:"hm"
        };
      }
    
      return null;
    }
    
    function combineStatuses(primary,edit){
      if(!primary && !edit){
        return {text:"",cls:"",reason:"",kind:""};
      }
    
      if(primary && !edit) return primary;
      if(!primary && edit) return edit;
    
      return {
        text:`${primary.text} | ${edit.text}`,
        cls:primary.kind==="double" || primary.kind==="error"
          ? primary.cls
          : "status-mixed",
        reason:`${primary.reason} ${edit.reason}`,
        kind:primary.kind
      };
    }
    
    function getRowStatus(item,index,forExport=false){
      const rowKey=String(item.id ?? `idx-${index}`);
    
      const dataError=getDataErrorStatus(item);
      const duplicate=STATUS_MAP.get(rowKey)||null;
      const anomaly=HM_ANOMALY_MAP.get(rowKey)||null;
      // Excluded history is an audit/reference policy, not an operational error.
      // Do not compare historical rows against an old stored hm_awal baseline.
      const visibleAnomaly=!forExport && anomaly?.kind!=="excluded" ? anomaly : null;
      return dataError || duplicate || visibleAnomaly || {text:"",cls:"",reason:"",kind:""};
    }
    
    async function fetchAllFuelHistory(){
      const pageSize=1000;
      let from=0, all=[];
      while(true){
        const {data,error}=await supabaseClient
          .from("fuel_history")
          .select("*")
          .order("tanggal",{ascending:true})
          .order("id",{ascending:true})
          .range(from,from+pageSize-1);
    
        if(error) throw error;
        all=all.concat(data||[]);
        if(!data || data.length<pageSize) break;
        from+=pageSize;
      }
      return all;
    }
    
    async function fetchAllAudit(){
      const pageSize=1000;
      let from=0, all=[];
    
      while(true){
        const {data,error}=await supabaseClient
          .from("fuel_history_audit")
          .select("id,created_at,action,record_id,changed_by_name,changed_by_role,old_data,new_data")
          .eq("action","UPDATE")
          .order("created_at",{ascending:true})
          .range(from,from+pageSize-1);
    
        if(error) throw error;
    
        all=all.concat(data||[]);
    
        if(!data || data.length<pageSize) break;
        from+=pageSize;
      }
    
      return all;
    }
    
    function updateDateMode(){
      const mode=document.getElementById("dateMode").value;
      document.getElementById("singleDateBox").classList.toggle("hidden",mode!=="single");
      document.getElementById("startDateBox").classList.toggle("hidden",mode!=="range");
      document.getElementById("endDateBox").classList.toggle("hidden",mode!=="range");
      renderFiltered();
    }
    
    function applyFilter(data){
      const mode=document.getElementById("dateMode").value;
      const single=document.getElementById("filterTanggal").value;
      const start=document.getElementById("filterTanggalAwal").value;
      const end=document.getElementById("filterTanggalAkhir").value;
      const shift=document.getElementById("filterShift").value;
      const wh=document.getElementById("filterWH").value;
      const fuelTruck=document.getElementById("filterFuelTruck").value;
      const unit=document.getElementById("filterUnit").value.trim().toUpperCase();
      const fuelman=document.getElementById("filterFuelman").value.trim().toUpperCase();
      const hmAudit=document.getElementById("filterHmAudit")?.value||"";
    
      return data.filter(item=>{
        const t=item.tanggal||"";
        if(mode==="single" && single && t!==single) return false;
        if(mode==="range" && start && t<start) return false;
        if(mode==="range" && end && t>end) return false;
        if(shift && String(item.shift)!==shift) return false;
        if(wh && getWH(item)!==wh) return false;
        if(fuelTruck && getFuelTruck(item)!==fuelTruck) return false;
        if(unit && !String(item.unit||"").toUpperCase().includes(unit)) return false;
        if(fuelman && !getFuelman(item).toUpperCase().includes(fuelman)) return false;
        if(hmAudit){
          const a=HM_ANOMALY_MAP.get(String(item.id??""));
          if(hmAudit==="anomaly" && !(a&&["missing","down","jump"].includes(a.kind)))return false;
          if(hmAudit==="missing" && a?.kind!=="missing")return false;
          if(hmAudit==="down" && a?.kind!=="down")return false;
          if(hmAudit==="jump" && a?.kind!=="jump")return false;
          if(hmAudit==="excluded" && item.hm_ref_excluded!==true)return false;
        }
        return true;
      });
    }
    
    function localIsoDate(date){
      const y=date.getFullYear();
      const m=String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0");
      const d=String(date.getDate()).padStart(2,"0");
      return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
    }
    
    function getOperationalMtdBounds(){return window.KPPTime.operationalMtdBounds();}
    
    function updateSummary(data,{isMtd=false,mtdBounds=null}={}){
      let total=0,s1=0,s2=0;
      const warehouseTotals=new Map();
      data.forEach(item=>{
        const q=Number(item.fuel)||0;
        total+=q;
        const wh=getWH(item);
        if(wh&&wh!=="-")warehouseTotals.set(wh,(warehouseTotals.get(wh)||0)+q);
        if(Number(item.shift)===1)s1+=q;
        if(Number(item.shift)===2)s2+=q;
      });
    
      const suffix=isMtd?" MTD":"";
      document.getElementById("totalFuelLabel").textContent="TOTAL QTY ISSUED"+suffix;
      document.getElementById("totalShift1Label").textContent="SHIFT 1"+suffix;
      document.getElementById("totalShift2Label").textContent="SHIFT 2"+suffix;
      document.getElementById("totalFuel").textContent=formatAngka(total)+" L";
      document.getElementById("totalShift1").textContent=formatAngka(s1)+" L";
      document.getElementById("totalShift2").textContent=formatAngka(s2)+" L";
    
      const host=document.getElementById("warehouseSummaryCards");
      host.innerHTML="";
      const whOrder=Array.from(new Set([...VALID_WHS,...warehouseTotals.keys()])).sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b));
      whOrder.forEach(wh=>{
        const card=document.createElement("div");
        card.className="summary-card";
        const label=document.createElement("div");
        label.className="summary-title";
        label.textContent=wh+suffix;
        const value=document.createElement("div");
        value.className="summary-value";
        value.textContent=formatAngka(warehouseTotals.get(wh)||0)+" L";
        card.append(label,value);
        host.appendChild(card);
      });
    
      const title=isMtd && mtdBounds
        ? `Ringkasan MTD operasional ${formatTanggal(mtdBounds.start)} - ${formatTanggal(mtdBounds.end)}`
        : "Ringkasan mengikuti periode/filter yang dipilih";
      document.querySelectorAll(".summary-card").forEach(card=>card.title=title);
    }
    
    function td(text,cls="",title=""){
      const cell=document.createElement("td");
      cell.textContent=text;
      if(cls) cell.className=cls;
      if(title) cell.title=title;
      return cell;
    }
    
    let HM_JALAN_MAP=new Map();
    
    function buildHmJalanMap(data){
      const map=new Map();
      const previousHmByUnit=new Map();
    
      (data||[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
        const unit=String(item?.unit||"").trim().toUpperCase();
        const rowKey=String(item?.id ?? `idx-${index}`);
        const hmRaw=item?.hm_akhir;
        const hm=hmRaw===null || hmRaw===undefined || hmRaw==="" ? null : Number(hmRaw);
    
        if(!unit || !Number.isFinite(hm)){
          map.set(rowKey,null);
          return;
        }
    
        const previous=previousHmByUnit.get(unit);
        let jalan=null;
    
        if(Number.isFinite(previous) && hm>=previous){
          jalan=hm-previous;
        }else if(!Number.isFinite(previous)){
          const stored=Number(item?.hm_jalan);
          if(item?.hm_jalan!==null && item?.hm_jalan!==undefined && item?.hm_jalan!=="" && Number.isFinite(stored) && stored>=0){
            jalan=stored;
          }
        }
    
        map.set(rowKey,jalan);
        previousHmByUnit.set(unit,hm);
      });
    
      return map;
    }
    
    function getHmJalan(item){
      const rowKey=String(item?.id ?? "");
      if(HM_JALAN_MAP.has(rowKey)) return HM_JALAN_MAP.get(rowKey);
    
      const stored=Number(item?.hm_jalan);
      if(item?.hm_jalan!==null && item?.hm_jalan!==undefined && item?.hm_jalan!=="" && Number.isFinite(stored) && stored>=0){
        return stored;
      }
    
      return null;
    }
    
    function renderTable(data){
      const body=document.getElementById("logsheetBody");
      body.innerHTML="";
    
      data.slice(0,LOGSHEET_VISIBLE_LIMIT).forEach((item,index)=>{
        const unit=String(item.unit||"").trim().toUpperCase();
        const m=MASTER_UNIT[unit]||{egi:"",ellipse:""};
        const hmJalan=getHmJalan(item);
        const row=document.createElement("tr");
    
        row.appendChild(td(item.id||index+1));
        row.appendChild(td(formatTanggal(item.tanggal)));
        row.appendChild(td(item.jam ? String(item.jam).slice(0,8) : "-"));
        row.appendChild(td(item.shift||"-"));
        row.appendChild(td(getWH(item)));
        row.appendChild(td(getFuelTruck(item)));
        row.appendChild(td(unit||"(KOSONG)"));
        row.appendChild(td(m.egi||"-","egi-cell"));
        row.appendChild(td(m.ellipse||unit||"-"));
        row.appendChild(td(item.hm_akhir!==null && item.hm_akhir!==undefined && item.hm_akhir!=="" ? formatAngka(item.hm_akhir) : "-"));
        row.appendChild(td(hmJalan!==null ? formatAngka(hmJalan) : "-"));
        row.appendChild(td(formatAngka(item.fuel),"qty"));
        row.appendChild(td(item.operator||"-"));
        row.appendChild(td(getFuelman(item)));
    
        const status=getRowStatus(item,index);
        const statusCell=document.createElement("td");
        if(status.cls)statusCell.className=status.cls;
        statusCell.title=status.reason||"";
        const wrap=document.createElement("div");
        wrap.className="status-cell-wrap";
        const label=document.createElement("span");
        label.textContent=status.text||"";
        wrap.appendChild(label);
        const anomaly=HM_ANOMALY_MAP.get(String(item.id??""));
        // Editing history must remain available even when its warning is hidden.
        // This page is restricted to management roles; the existing RPC enforces write access.
        if(item.id!==null && item.id!==undefined){
          const btn=document.createElement("button");
          btn.type="button";
          btn.className="cleanup-btn";
          btn.textContent="🧹 RAPIKAN HM";
          btn.title=`Rapikan HM ${unit} — Log No. ${item.id}`;
          btn.addEventListener("click",()=>openHmCleanup(item,anomaly));
          wrap.appendChild(btn);
        }
        statusCell.appendChild(wrap);
        row.appendChild(statusCell);
    
        body.appendChild(row);
      });
    }
    
    function updateLogsheetPaging(total){
      const textEl=document.getElementById("logsheetPagingText");
      const button=document.getElementById("loadMoreLogsheet");
      if(!textEl||!button)return;
      const shown=Math.min(LOGSHEET_VISIBLE_LIMIT,total);
      textEl.textContent=total
        ? `Menampilkan ${formatAngka(shown)} dari ${formatAngka(total)} transaksi hasil filter.`
        : "";
      button.classList.toggle("hidden",shown>=total);
    }
    
    function loadMoreLogsheet(){
      LOGSHEET_VISIBLE_LIMIT+=LOGSHEET_PAGE_SIZE;
      renderFiltered(false);
    }
    
    function renderFiltered(resetWindow=true){
      if(resetWindow)LOGSHEET_VISIBLE_LIMIT=LOGSHEET_PAGE_SIZE;
      HM_ANOMALY_MAP=buildHmAnomalyMap(ALL_DATA);
      const filtered=applyFilter(ALL_DATA);
    
      const dateMode=document.getElementById("dateMode").value;
      if(dateMode==="all"){
        const mtdBounds=getOperationalMtdBounds();
        const mtdData=filtered.filter(item=>{
          const t=String(item.tanggal||"");
          return t>=mtdBounds.start && t<=mtdBounds.end;
        });
        updateSummary(mtdData,{isMtd:true,mtdBounds});
      }else{
        updateSummary(filtered,{isMtd:false});
      }
    
      renderTable(filtered);
      updateLogsheetPaging(filtered.length);
    
      const loading=document.getElementById("loading");
      if(filtered.length===0){
        loading.style.display="block";
        loading.textContent="Tidak ada data untuk filter yang dipilih.";
      } else {
        loading.style.display="none";
      }
    
      const statusCounts=filtered.reduce((acc,item,index)=>{
        const st=getRowStatus(item,index);
    
        if(st.kind==="double") acc.double++;
        if(st.kind==="error") acc.error++;
        if(st.kind==="hm") acc.hm++;
        const anomaly=HM_ANOMALY_MAP.get(String(item.id??""));
        if(anomaly?.kind==="missing") acc.hmMissing++;
        if(anomaly?.kind==="down") acc.hmDown++;
        if(anomaly?.kind==="jump") acc.hmJump++;
        if(item.hm_ref_excluded===true) acc.excluded++;
        if(getEditStatus(item)) acc.edit++;
    
        return acc;
      },{double:0,error:0,hm:0,hmMissing:0,hmDown:0,hmJump:0,excluded:0,edit:0});
    
      const dataNotice=document.getElementById("dataNotice");
      if(dataNotice) dataNotice.textContent=
        `Database: ${formatAngka(ALL_DATA.length)} transaksi • `+
        `Filter: ${formatAngka(filtered.length)} • `+
        `DOUBLE: ${formatAngka(statusCounts.double)} • `+
        `ERROR DATA: ${formatAngka(statusCounts.error)} • `+
        `HM KOSONG: ${formatAngka(statusCounts.hmMissing)} • `+
        `HM TURUN: ${formatAngka(statusCounts.hmDown)} • `+
        `HM LONCAT: ${formatAngka(statusCounts.hmJump)} • `+
        `HISTORI DIKECUALIKAN: ${formatAngka(statusCounts.excluded)} • `+
        `EDIT ADMIN/GL: ${formatAngka(statusCounts.edit)}.`;
    
      return filtered;
    }
    
    async function loadData(){
      const loading=document.getElementById("loading");
      const errorBox=document.getElementById("errorBox");
    
      loading.style.display="block";
      loading.textContent="Mengambil seluruh data dari database...";
      errorBox.style.display="none";
    
      try{
        await Promise.all([loadStorageMaster(),loadUnitMasterOnline()]);
    
        const [fuelRows,auditRows]=await Promise.all([
          fetchAllFuelHistory(),
          fetchAllAudit().catch(error=>{
            console.warn("Audit perubahan belum terbaca:",error.message);
            return [];
          })
        ]);
    
        ALL_DATA=fuelRows;
        HM_JALAN_MAP=buildHmJalanMap(ALL_DATA);
        populateStorageFilters(ALL_DATA);
        AUDIT_MAP=buildAuditMap(auditRows);
        STATUS_MAP=buildStatusMap(ALL_DATA);
        HM_ANOMALY_MAP=buildHmAnomalyMap(ALL_DATA);
    
        renderFiltered();
      }catch(error){
        console.error(error);
        loading.style.display="none";
        errorBox.style.display="block";
        errorBox.textContent="❌ Gagal mengambil data logsheet: "+error.message;
      }
    }
    
    function openHmCleanup(item,anomaly){
      HM_CLEANUP_ROW=item;
      document.getElementById("hmCleanupContext").textContent=`${String(item.unit||"-").toUpperCase()} • ${formatTanggal(item.tanggal)} • Shift ${item.shift||"-"} • ${anomaly?.text||"HM histori"}`;
      document.getElementById("cleanupOldHm").value=item.hm_akhir??"";
      document.getElementById("cleanupNewHm").value=item.hm_akhir??"";
      document.getElementById("cleanupReason").value="";
      document.getElementById("hmCleanupModal").classList.remove("hidden");
      setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById("cleanupNewHm")?.focus(),50);
    }
    function closeHmCleanup(){
      document.getElementById("hmCleanupModal").classList.add("hidden");
      HM_CLEANUP_ROW=null;
    }
    async function saveHmCleanup(){
      if(!HM_CLEANUP_ROW)return;
      const newHmRaw=document.getElementById("cleanupNewHm").value;
      const reason=document.getElementById("cleanupReason").value.trim();
      const newHm=newHmRaw===""?null:Number(newHmRaw);
      const currentHm=HM_CLEANUP_ROW.hm_akhir;
      const currentIsBlank=currentHm===null||currentHm===undefined||currentHm==="";
      if(currentIsBlank && newHm===null){alert("HM histori masih kosong. Masukkan HM yang benar terlebih dahulu.");return}
      if(newHm!==null&&(!Number.isFinite(newHm)||newHm<0)){alert("HM baru tidak valid.");return}
      const btn=document.getElementById("cleanupSaveBtn");
      btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent="MENYIMPAN...";
      try{
        const {data,error}=await supabaseClient.rpc("kpp_cleanup_history_hm",{
          p_record_id:Number(HM_CLEANUP_ROW.id),
          p_new_hm:newHm,
          p_reason:reason||null
        });
        if(error)throw error;
        closeHmCleanup();
        await loadData();
        alert(`✅ HM histori ${data?.unit||""} berhasil dirapikan.\n\nBaris ini tidak akan memengaruhi HM reference.`);
      }catch(err){
        alert("❌ Gagal merapikan HM: "+(err?.message||err));
      }finally{
        btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent="SIMPAN HISTORI AMAN";
      }
    }
    
    async function downloadExcel(){
      const data=applyFilter(ALL_DATA);
    
      if(data.length===0){
        alert("Tidak ada data untuk didownload.");
        return;
      }
    
      const btn=document.getElementById("downloadBtn");
      const originalText=btn.textContent;
      btn.disabled=true;
      btn.textContent="⏳ MEMBUAT EXCEL...";
    
      try{
        const workbook=new ExcelJS.Workbook();
        workbook.creator="KPP Fuel Management System";
        workbook.company="PT. KALIMANTAN PRIMA PERSADA";
        workbook.subject="Daily Fuel By Logsheet";
        workbook.created=new Date();
    
        const ws=workbook.addWorksheet("LOGSHEET FUEL",{
          views:[{state:"frozen",ySplit:9}]
        });
    
        const headers=[
          "LOG NO",
          "TANGGAL",
          "JAM",
          "SHIFT",
          "WH",
          "FUEL TRUCK",
          "CODE UNIT",
          "EGI",
          "CODE ELLIPS",
          "HM",
          "HM JALAN",
          "QTY ISSUED",
          "OPERATOR UNIT",
          "STD + Fuelman",
          "Cek"
        ];
    
        // Lebar kolom tetap mengikuti bentuk logsheet lama, dengan Fuel Truck ditambahkan.
        const widths=[11,13,10,7,9,12,13,34,16,12,12,14,22,17,55];
        widths.forEach((width,index)=>{
          ws.getColumn(index+1).width=width;
        });
    
        // ===== IDENTITAS / JUDUL - mengikuti gaya logsheet lama =====
        ws.mergeCells("A2:O2");
        const companyCell=ws.getCell("A2");
        companyCell.value="PT. KALIMANTAN PRIMA PERSADA";
        companyCell.font={
          name:"Times New Roman",
          size:20,
          bold:true,
          color:{argb:"FF00B050"}
        };
        companyCell.alignment={vertical:"middle",horizontal:"left"};
    
        ws.mergeCells("A3:O3");
        const subtitleCell=ws.getCell("A3");
        subtitleCell.value="Integrated Mining Services";
        subtitleCell.font={
          name:"Times New Roman",
          size:16,
          italic:true,
          color:{argb:"FF000000"}
        };
        subtitleCell.alignment={vertical:"middle",horizontal:"left"};
    
        ws.mergeCells("A5:O5");
        const titleCell=ws.getCell("A5");
    
        const mode=document.getElementById("dateMode").value;
        let periodText="SEMUA PERIODE";
    
        if(mode==="single"){
          const d=document.getElementById("filterTanggal").value;
          periodText=d ? formatTanggal(d) : "TANGGAL TERPILIH";
        }
    
        if(mode==="range"){
          const awal=document.getElementById("filterTanggalAwal").value;
          const akhir=document.getElementById("filterTanggalAkhir").value;
          periodText=
            (awal ? formatTanggal(awal) : "AWAL")+
            " - "+
            (akhir ? formatTanggal(akhir) : "AKHIR");
        }
    
        titleCell.value="DAILY FUEL BY LOGSHEET PERIODE "+periodText;
        titleCell.font={
          name:"Algerian",
          size:24,
          bold:true,
          italic:true,
          color:{argb:"FF00B050"}
        };
        titleCell.alignment={vertical:"middle",horizontal:"center"};
    
        // Total Qty Issued diletakkan tepat di kolom QTY ISSUED seperti logsheet lama.
        const totalFuel=data.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+(Number(item.fuel)||0),0);
        const totalCell=ws.getCell("L7");
        totalCell.value=totalFuel;
        totalCell.numFmt="0";
        totalCell.font={name:"Calibri",size:16,bold:true,color:{argb:"FF000000"}};
        totalCell.fill={type:"pattern",pattern:"solid",fgColor:{argb:"FFFFFF00"}};
        totalCell.alignment={vertical:"middle",horizontal:"center"};
    
        // ===== KETERANGAN STATUS =====
        ws.getCell("A8").value="KETERANGAN";
        ws.mergeCells("B8:E8");
        ws.getCell("B8").value="JINGGA = ERROR DATA";
        ws.mergeCells("F8:I8");
        ws.getCell("F8").value="MERAH = DOUBLE POSTING";
        ws.mergeCells("J8:O8");
        ws.getCell("J8").value="";
    
        const gold="FFBF9000";
        const legendRanges=["A8:A8","B8:E8","F8:I8","J8:O8"];
    
        legendRanges.forEach(range=>{
          const r=ws.getCell(range.split(":")[0]);
          r.font={name:"Calibri",size:12,bold:true,color:{argb:"FF000000"}};
          r.alignment={vertical:"middle",horizontal:"center"};
        });
    
        // Beri border emas pada seluruh baris keterangan.
        for(let col=1;col<=15;col++){
          const c=ws.getCell(8,col);
          c.border={
            top:{style:"medium",color:{argb:gold}},
            left:{style:"medium",color:{argb:gold}},
            bottom:{style:"medium",color:{argb:gold}},
            right:{style:"medium",color:{argb:gold}}
          };
        }
    
        ws.getCell("B8").fill={type:"pattern",pattern:"solid",fgColor:{argb:"FFF4B183"}};
        ws.getCell("F8").fill={type:"pattern",pattern:"solid",fgColor:{argb:"FFFF0000"}};
        ws.getCell("F8").font={name:"Calibri",size:12,bold:true,color:{argb:"FFFFFFFF"}};
        ws.getCell("J8").fill={type:"pattern",pattern:"solid",fgColor:{argb:"FFFFFFFF"}};
    
        // ===== HEADER KOLOM =====
        const headerRow=ws.getRow(9);
        headers.forEach((header,index)=>{
          const cell=headerRow.getCell(index+1);
          cell.value=header;
          cell.font={
            name:"Calibri",
            size:11,
            bold:false,
            color:{argb:"FFFFFFFF"}
          };
          cell.fill={
            type:"pattern",
            pattern:"solid",
            fgColor:{argb:"FF000000"}
          };
          cell.alignment={
            vertical:"middle",
            horizontal:"center",
            wrapText:false
          };
          cell.border={
            top:{style:"medium",color:{argb:gold}},
            left:{style:"medium",color:{argb:gold}},
            bottom:{style:"medium",color:{argb:gold}},
            right:{style:"medium",color:{argb:gold}}
          };
        });
    
        headerRow.height=22;
    
        // ===== DATA =====
        data.forEach((item,index)=>{
          const unit=String(item.unit||"").trim().toUpperCase();
          const m=MASTER_UNIT[unit]||{egi:"",ellipse:""};
          const rowStatus=getRowStatus(item,index,true);
          const status=rowStatus.text;
    
          const row=ws.addRow([
            item.id||index+1,
            item.tanggal ? (() => {
              const [y,m,d]=String(item.tanggal).split("-").map(Number);
              return new Date(Date.UTC(y,m-1,d));
            })() : "",
            item.jam ? String(item.jam).slice(0,8) : "",
            item.shift||"",
            getWH(item),
            getFuelTruck(item)==="-" ? "" : getFuelTruck(item),
            unit,
            m.egi||"",
            m.ellipse||unit,
            item.hm_akhir ?? "",
            getHmJalan(item) ?? "",
            Number(item.fuel)||0,
            item.operator||"",
            getFuelman(item)==="-" ? "" : getFuelman(item),
            status
          ]);
    
          row.height=20;
    
          row.eachCell({includeEmpty:true},cell=>{
            cell.font={
              name:"Calibri",
              size:11,
              color:{argb:"FF000000"}
            };
            cell.alignment={
              vertical:"middle",
              horizontal:"center",
              wrapText:false
            };
            cell.border={
              bottom:{style:"hair",color:{argb:"FFD9D9D9"}}
            };
          });
    
          // Format tanggal, HM, dan Qty.
          row.getCell(2).numFmt="dd/mm/yyyy";
          row.getCell(10).numFmt="0.##";
          row.getCell(11).numFmt="0.##";
          row.getCell(12).numFmt="0";
    
          // Kolom EGI dibuat tetap rapi walau nama panjang.
          row.getCell(8).alignment={
            vertical:"middle",
            horizontal:"center",
            wrapText:true
          };
    
          // Status sesuai aturan KPP-FMS sekarang.
          const statusCell=row.getCell(15);
    
          if(rowStatus.kind==="double"){
            statusCell.fill={
              type:"pattern",
              pattern:"solid",
              fgColor:{argb:"FFFF0000"}
            };
            statusCell.font={
              name:"Calibri",
              size:11,
              bold:true,
              color:{argb:"FFFFFFFF"}
            };
          }else if(rowStatus.kind==="error"){
            statusCell.fill={
              type:"pattern",
              pattern:"solid",
              fgColor:{argb:"FFF4B183"}
            };
            statusCell.font={
              name:"Calibri",
              size:11,
              bold:true,
              color:{argb:"FF000000"}
            };
          }else if(rowStatus.kind==="down" || rowStatus.kind==="jump"){
            statusCell.fill={type:"pattern",pattern:"solid",fgColor:{argb:"FFFFE4E6"}};
            statusCell.font={name:"Calibri",size:11,bold:true,color:{argb:"FFBE123C"}};
          }else if(rowStatus.kind==="excluded"){
            statusCell.fill={type:"pattern",pattern:"solid",fgColor:{argb:"FFE2E8F0"}};
            statusCell.font={name:"Calibri",size:11,bold:true,color:{argb:"FF334155"}};
          }else if(rowStatus.kind==="hm"){
            statusCell.fill={
              type:"pattern",
              pattern:"solid",
              fgColor:{argb:"FFFFEB9C"}
            };
            statusCell.font={
              name:"Calibri",
              size:11,
              bold:true,
              color:{argb:"FF9C6500"}
            };
          }else if(rowStatus.kind==="edit"){
            statusCell.fill={
              type:"pattern",
              pattern:"solid",
              fgColor:{argb:"FFD9EAF7"}
            };
            statusCell.font={
              name:"Calibri",
              size:11,
              bold:true,
              color:{argb:"FF1D4ED8"}
            };
          }else{
            statusCell.value="";
            statusCell.fill={
              type:"pattern",
              pattern:"solid",
              fgColor:{argb:"FFFFFFFF"}
            };
          }
        });
    
        // Filter seperti Excel biasa.
        ws.autoFilter={
          from:{row:9,column:1},
          to:{row:9,column:14}
        };
    
        // Print / tampilan Excel lebih enak.
        ws.pageSetup={
          orientation:"landscape",
          fitToPage:true,
          fitToWidth:1,
          fitToHeight:0,
          paperSize:9,
          margins:{
            left:0.25,
            right:0.25,
            top:0.5,
            bottom:0.5,
            header:0.2,
            footer:0.2
          }
        };
    
        ws.pageSetup.printTitlesRow="1:9";
        ws.properties.defaultRowHeight=20;
    
        let suffix="SEMUA_TANGGAL";
        if(mode==="single"){
          suffix=document.getElementById("filterTanggal").value||"TANGGAL";
        }
        if(mode==="range"){
          suffix=
            (document.getElementById("filterTanggalAwal").value||"AWAL")+
            "_sd_"+
            (document.getElementById("filterTanggalAkhir").value||"AKHIR");
        }
    
        const buffer=await workbook.xlsx.writeBuffer();
        const blob=new Blob(
          [buffer],
          {type:"application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet"}
        );
    
        const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const link=document.createElement("a");
        link.href=url;
        link.download="KPP_Fuel_Logsheet_"+suffix+".xlsx";
        document.body.appendChild(link);
        link.click();
        link.remove();
        setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
    
      }catch(error){
        console.error(error);
        alert("Gagal membuat Excel: "+error.message);
      }finally{
        btn.disabled=false;
        btn.textContent=originalText;
      }
    }
    
    function allKnownFuelTrucks(){
      const codes=new Set(
        LOG_STORAGE_ROWS
          .filter(row=>String(row.storage_type||"").toUpperCase()==="FT")
          .map(row=>String(row.code||"").trim().toUpperCase())
          .filter(Boolean)
      );
      ALL_DATA.forEach(item=>{
        const code=String(item.fuel_truck||"").trim().toUpperCase();
        if(code)codes.add(code);
      });
      return Array.from(codes).sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b));
    }
    
    function setFilterOptions(select,values,allLabel,preferred=""){
      select.innerHTML="";
      const all=document.createElement("option");
      all.value="";all.textContent=allLabel;select.appendChild(all);
      values.forEach(value=>{
        const option=document.createElement("option");
        option.value=value;option.textContent=value;select.appendChild(option);
      });
      if(preferred&&values.includes(preferred))select.value=preferred;
    }
    
    function fuelTrucksForWh(wh){
      return allKnownFuelTrucks().filter(code=>{
        if(LOG_FT_MAP[code]===wh)return true;
        return ALL_DATA.some(item=>String(item.fuel_truck||"").trim().toUpperCase()===code&&getWH(item)===wh);
      });
    }
    
    function syncFuelTruckFromWH(preferred=""){
      const whSelect=document.getElementById("filterWH");
      const ftSelect=document.getElementById("filterFuelTruck");
      const hint=document.getElementById("ftLockHint");
      const wh=whSelect.value;
      const codes=wh?fuelTrucksForWh(wh):allKnownFuelTrucks();
      setFilterOptions(ftSelect,codes,wh?"Semua FT di "+wh:"Semua FT",preferred);
    
      const lock=Boolean(wh&&codes.length===1);
      if(lock)ftSelect.value=codes[0];
      ftSelect.disabled=lock;
      ftSelect.classList.toggle("ft-locked",lock);
      ftSelect.title=lock?`${wh} memakai ${codes[0]}`:"";
      if(hint)hint.textContent=lock?`• ${codes[0]}`:"";
    }
    
    function populateStorageFilters(data){
      const whSelect=document.getElementById("filterWH");
      const ftSelect=document.getElementById("filterFuelTruck");
      const oldWh=whSelect.value;
      const oldFt=ftSelect.value;
      const whs=new Set(VALID_WHS);
      (data||[]).forEach(item=>{
        const wh=getWH(item);
        if(wh&&wh!=="-")whs.add(wh);
      });
      const values=Array.from(whs).sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b));
      setFilterOptions(whSelect,values,"Semua WH",oldWh);
      syncFuelTruckFromWH(oldFt);
    }
    
    ["filterTanggal","filterTanggalAwal","filterTanggalAkhir","filterShift"]
